Clear any layout cache; will be re-removed later anyway.\n                        remove_layout_caches_from_paragraph(new_p)\n                        parent.insert(insert_pos, new_p)\n                        insert_pos += 1\n                        numbered.append(new_p)\n\n                elif len(followup_items) < len(numbered):\n                    # Python script to perform the update\n\nCreated: `/tmp/update_hwpx.py`\n\nKey behaviors implemented per spec:\n\n- **3a Namespace handling:** scans raw root tag for all `xmlns` / `xmlns:prefix` declarations and registers them with `ET.register_namespace()` before parsing.\n- **3b Load update data:** loads JSON + CSV; CSV sorted numerically by `sequence`.\n- **3c Text replacement:** walks all elements and replaces old → new values (old values are extracted from the actual XML content, not guessed). Explicitly avoids touching the appendix sentence.\n- **3d Follow-up lines:** detects “후속 조치” section and replaces the consecutive numbered follow-up paragraphs with CSV items in order; adds/removes paragraphs as needed by cloning/removing.\n- **3e Remove stale layout caches:** removes `<hp:lineSegArray>`, `<hp:linesegarray>`, and `<hp:lineseg>` in any modified `<hp:p>`.\n- **3f Write back XML:** writes `UTF-8` with XML declaration.\n- **3g Repackage:** writes `/root/renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx` as a ZIP; adds `mimetype` first if present (not present in this archive).\n\nNote: in this playbook, follow-ups were actually located in `Contents/section1.xml`, so the script updates follow-ups there (and still updates renewal window text there too). `section0.xml` contains the main header fields and table, and is updated accordingly.\n\n\n## Step 4: Run the script\n\nExecuted:\n\n- `python3 /tmp/update_hwpx.py`\n\nResult:\n\n- Created `/root/renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx`
